{"parentUuid":"ec83b823-246d-440a-84c5-ab926d1920c0","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"5154689d-10c1-4b92-9ac5-e24844095e46","timestamp":"2026-05-09T07:10:25.990Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d445a8b-bf27-47e3-8d5e-cf5f511500fa","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"5154689d-10c1-4b92-9ac5-e24844095e46","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- empirical-probe-first: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- ccs:continue: Continue last CCS delegation session [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- ccs: Delegate task with intelligent profile selection [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- autonomous: /autonomous — Toggle Autonomous Mode (on/off/status)\n- budget-check: /budget-check — Current Session Token Budget Status\n- devils-advocate: /devils-advocate — Adversarial Critique\n- drift-check: /drift-check — Run Drift Signals DR1-DR12\n- drill-me: /drill-me — Interactive Ubiquitous Language Extraction\n- grill-me: /grill-me — Relentless Plan/Design Interview\n- handoff-read: /handoff-read — Lightweight Last Session Load\n- master-plan: /master-plan — High-Level Work Decomposition\n- session-end: /session-end — Close Work Session\n- session-start: /session-start — Begin Work Session\n- session-verify: /session-verify — Mid-Session Alignment Check\n- spec-author: /spec-author — Create Dual-Layer Specification\n- spec-to-wiki: /spec-to-wiki — Convert Raw Spec to Obsidian Wiki Format\n- ul-audit: /ul-audit — Audit Ubiquitous Language Consistency\n- vbw-check: /vbw-check — Apply Verify-Before-Write Protocol\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":51,"isInitial":true},"type":"attachment","uuid":"51a7d211-58bc-4fd6-9aa7-41ced9de8d8d","timestamp":"2026-05-09T07:10:25.990Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d445a8b-bf27-47e3-8d5e-cf5f511500fa","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"125ee82a-14ef-49aa-ae3c-5da98ebf1cc8","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# agent-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`agent-workspace/` is the **agent-owned execution + memory** layer. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. The two communicate through:\n- Human → Agent: `human-workspace/user_prompt/`, `human-workspace/decisions/`\n- Agent → Human: `human-workspace/notifications/`, `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`\n- Bidirectional: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/` — agent writes pending; either human or agent (per Auto-mv rule below) moves resolved bundles to answered/\n- Audit trail: this directory's `memory/decisions/` references `human-workspace/` source files via path pointers\n\n**Auto-mv rule (HH-E.2 — D-031 ratification, 2026-05-05)**: Agent MAY mv a bundle from `q-and-a/pending/` to `q-and-a/answered/` IFF ALL of the following hold:\n\n1. **Frontmatter signal**: bundle frontmatter `status:` field value starts with one of: `answered-`, `closed-`, `resolved-`. Examples already in repo: `answered-via-chat`, `answered-via-AskUserQuestion`, `answered-2026-05-04-via-chat`. Detection is deterministic (head -20 of file + grep `^status:`).\n2. **No human-veto signal**: bundle frontmatter has NO `wait_until:` ISO-8601 timestamp greater than current epoch — if present, agent MUST defer mv until that timestamp passes. Allows human to override auto-mv per-bundle without contract amendment.\n3. **No global pause**: file `human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused` does NOT exist — global kill switch for the auto-mv mechanism (single empty file presence pauses all auto-mv).\n4. **Hook validation**: the mv is performed by `scripts/hooks/qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` (Stop hook). Direct manual `mv` invocation by agent (e.g. via Bash tool) is STILL forbidden — only the validated hook path is authorized.\n\nAgent never writes to `human-workspace/` outside the designated `q-and-a/pending/` write channel + the auto-mv rule above + `notifications/` write channel (existing).\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"7e0a758e-1074-4d85-97a6-e90bfe68ee59","timestamp":"2026-05-09T07:10:32.038Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d445a8b-bf27-47e3-8d5e-cf5f511500fa","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"6c7f8dfb-6591-4f20-95e0-ee261065d28e","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# human-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents reading anything inside `human-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`human-workspace/` is the **human-authored authority** layer. Everything here represents **human decisions, scope, intent, and verification**. Agents READ this directory as authoritative; agents do NOT WRITE here except in one designated channel (`q-and-a/pending/` — where agents post questions for human to answer).\n\nThis separation exists because of a failure mode observed in the orch sister project (orch `decisions/040`, post-mortem CF-DOGFOOD-2): when human and agent share a single workspace without contract, agent silently overrides human-CRITICAL items via technical-defer logic. The user explicitly raised this concern in `human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_02_init.txt` §1.1.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Who writes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `user_prompt/` | Human's mid-flight scope/idea drops. Naming: `YYYYMMDD_NN_<slug>.txt`. Files are immutable post-creation (audit trail). | **Human only**. Agent: read-only. |\n| `decisions/` | Human's strategic decisions (e.g., scope-lock, charter-revision-trigger, peer-share authorization). Format: `D-H-<NNN>-<slug>.md`. | **Human only**. Agent: read-only. |\n| `q-and-a/pending/` | **The ONE channel where agent writes inside human-workspace**. Agent drops Q&A bundles per Grill Maximization doctrine; human responds inline or moves to `answered/`. | **Both** (agent writes new bundles; human writes answers). |\n| `q-and-a/answered/` | Q&A bundles human has answered (with answers inline). Agent reads answers and acts. Archive forever; provenance trail. | **Human moves** (drag from `pending/`); agent reads. |\n| `q-and-a/stale/` | Pending bundles that exceeded their `expected_answer_by` (default 24h, urgent 4h). Auto-moved by SessionStart hook (Track 5). | **Hook moves**; both read. |\n| `notifications/` | Agent push to human — summaries, alerts, milestones. Format: `N-<TS>-<level>-<slug>.md`. Levels: INFO / SUMMARY / ALERT. | **Agent writes**; human reads. |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Agent NEVER writes to `user_prompt/` or `decisions/`.** Permission deny. If something feels like a human decision but the human hasn't written it, dispatch a Q&A bundle into `q-and-a/pending/` instead.\n\n2. **`user_prompt/` files are immutable post-creation.** Agent never deletes, renames, or edits these files. They are the audit trail of human interventions.\n\n3. **Q&A bundle format** (per Track 4 spec): YAML frontmatter (`id`, `topic`, `opened_at`, `expected_answer_by`, `priority`, `related_decisions`, `status`) + question body + answer-fill section. Each bundle = up to 25 questions per Grill Maximization doctrine; split if more.\n\n4. **Agent does not move files between `q-and-a/{pending,answered,stale}/`** — that's human's role (or hook's, in case of stale). Agent only WRITES new pending bundles and READS answers.\n\n5. **Notifications are read-only for human.** Human shouldn't edit `notifications/*` files; if disagreement with notification content, drop a `user_prompt/` instead. Notifications are the agent's account of events.\n\n6. **Provenance pointers**: every `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-<NNN>` should reference its source `human-workspace/user_prompt/<file>` or `human-workspace/decisions/D-H-<NNN>` if the agent's decision derives from human input.\n\n7. **No multi-tenancy in this directory.** Per identity-scope.md (Track 7), stockforge is single-tenant Phase 0-5; if peer share happens it's via git-fork, each peer has their own `human-workspace/` per fork.\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nWhen loading session context (per `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` Track 7):\n\n1. **All `user_prompt/*.txt`** — every prompt, not just most recent. Per orch lesson (decision-040 USER-CRITICAL tier): never silently defer human-CRITICAL items.\n2. **All `decisions/*.md`** — human's strategic decisions are above charter principles in priority; charter is below human (charter was authored by human in the first place).\n3. **Latest `q-and-a/answered/*`** — recent answers update agent's understanding.\n4. **Open `q-and-a/pending/*`** — flag if waiting on human answers.\n5. **Recent `notifications/*`** (last 7 days) — for situational awareness.\n\n---\n\n## Agent Self-Check\n\nBefore writing to `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`, ask:\n- Is this question genuinely something the human must decide? (Charter principle / Charter-affecting / SCOPE-affecting / above ARCH-tier confidence threshold)\n- Have I already grilled this in a recent bundle? (Don't ask twice; don't pollute pending/)\n- Am I bundling per Grill Maximization (target 15-20 questions, max 25)? Or splintering across many small bundles?\n\nIf the answer to any is \"I'm not sure\" — DON'T write the bundle. Either answer it yourself with charter-principle reasoning + log decision, or wait until ambiguity clears.\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"31be17ba-28d9-4165-be38-7b93f77ff212","timestamp":"2026-05-09T07:11:09.251Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d445a8b-bf27-47e3-8d5e-cf5f511500fa","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

requires explicit user expedite override.\n81\t- **C**: Approve principle but amend wording — user provides preferred phrasing (e.g., shorten / different framing / move to Anti-Charter section / etc).\n82\t- **D**: Defer T8 to Phase 4+ — collect 30+ more days of harness-health-self-scan firing telemetry before charter promotion.\n83\t\n84\t---\n85\t\n86\t## Q3 — T6 charter-tier: Approve harness-health-self-scan.sh hook design?\n87\t\n88\t**Proposed design (per Phase 3.5 plan §T6.1-T6.5)**:\n89\t- Trigger: UserPromptSubmit chain (every prompt) + SessionStart chain (each session entry)\n90\t- Perf budget: <2s on UserPromptSubmit (must not bottleneck typing flow)\n91\t- Output: aggregates FAILs across HH-1..HH-12 signals; emits via `<system-reminder>` channel (UserPromptSubmit) or stderr (SessionStart)\n92\t- Optimization: cheap-first ordering (file mtime checks before grep on 1MB log); cache results across same-session prompts; suppress known-known issues (e.g., KI-S49b-1 Stop-hook Windows quirk)\n93\t- Companion firing-test: synthesizes ≥1 deliberate FAIL state (e.g., touches Tier 1 to bloat past 8K) and asserts hook detects + reports\n94\t\n95\t**Why charter-tier (not IMPL-tier)**: hook fires on EVERY prompt; affects routine LLM context; charter implications (Tier 1 ceiling adjacent + system-reminder pollution risk).\n96\t\n97\t- **A**: ✅ Approve as designed **(Recommended)** — full UserPromptSubmit + SessionStart coverage; <2s perf budget; defense-in-depth for empirical-firing.\n98\t- **B**: Approve with reduced scope (Stop-only hook, NOT UserPromptSubmit) — lower perf overhead; trade-off: misses mid-session FAILs.\n99\t- **C**: Approve BUT defer authoring to S+1 — depends T5 ratify first; T6 needs T5 protocol locked.\n100\t- **D**: Defer T6 entirely — author harness-health-self-scan only after Phase 4 empirical baseline.\n101\t\n102\t---\n103\t\n104\t## Q4 — Sequencing scope: After T5+T6+T8 ratified, what work order for S174+?\n105\t\n106\t**Why-needed**: Decides which residue/follow-on work runs FIRST after charter user-gate clears. SCOPE-tier (multi-track ordering decision affecting Phase 3 resume timing).\n107\t\n108\t- **A**: ✅ HH-G portability close FIRST **(Recommended)** — HH-G.1 general-harness CLAUDE.md template + HH-G.3 /attach smoke against fresh dir (`C:/htdocs/test-harness-portability/`); ~80-120K main; closes Phase 2.5 last 2 NOT-STARTED deliverables.\n109\t- **B**: M-S171-1 prevention hooks FIRST — idle-escape-detector.sh + phase-status-coherence.sh; ~60-80K main; harness self-protection against repeat of 22-session ROUTINE-IDLE waste pattern (M-S171-1 documented; high-recurrence risk if not authored).\n110\t- **C**: Phase 3 Track I Bayesian calibration FIRST — 008-S45 sub-plan; advances product roadmap (KOL calibration data); requires Phase 3.5 close validation first.\n111\t- **D**: Phase 3 Tracks J+K BC-7 sentiment+pump FIRST — 009-S51 sub-plan (D-032 architecture ratified S65); largest scope sub-plan; PAUSED awaiting Phase 3.5 close.\n112\t\n113\t---\n114\t\n115\t## Answer-fill section (human writes inline)\n116\t\n117\t**Q1 answer**: ____ (A/B/C/D + optional notes)\n118\t\n119\t**Q2 answer**: ____ (A/B/C/D + optional notes)\n120\t\n121\t**Q3 answer**: ____ (A/B/C/D + optional notes)\n122\t\n123\t**Q4 answer**: ____ (A/B/C/D + optional notes)\n124\t\n125\t---\n126\t\n127\t## Auto-mv eligibility\n128\t\n129\tPer HH-E.2 (D-031) Auto-mv rule:\n130\t- Status field MUST start with `answered-` / `closed-` / `resolved-` for hook to mv → answered/\n131\t- No `wait_until:` future timestamp present\n132\t- No `.auto-mv-paused` global kill switch\n133\t- Hook `qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` performs the mv (not direct Bash)\n134\t\n135\tWhen all 4 answers received: human (or auto-mv hook based on frontmatter status update) moves bundle pending/ → answered/. {"parentUuid":"b26b5d8d-32e4-4653-9e83-5460f21f1537","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"bf1874d2-a7bb-4600-be41-758be138a42a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016wW37921kqZMUwVKKw3jTS","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/usr/bin/env bash\n2\t# harness-health-self-scan.sh — continuous harness-health verification.\n3\t# Implements 12-signal catalog HH-1..HH-12 codified in agent-workspace/{proposals,constitution}/harness-health-protocol.md\n4\t# (currently at proposals/; mv to constitution/ pending M-S173-1 deny-lift workaround).\n5\t# Triggers: UserPromptSubmit (every prompt; same-session caching) + SessionStart (each session entry).\n6\t# Phase 3.5 T6 deliverable; D-035 ratified S173 via AskUserQuestion Q3=A.\n7\t# <2s perf budget on UserPromptSubmit. Exit 0 ALWAYS (informational, not blocking).\n8\tset -uo pipefail\n9\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n10\t\n11\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n12\tSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\n13\tmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n14\tHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n15\tHEALTH_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health.log\"\n16\tCACHE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health-cache-${SID}\"\n17\tTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n18\t\n19\t# === Same-session cache check (5-min TTL) ===\n20\tif [ -f \"$CACHE\" ]; then\n21\t  CACHE_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n22\t  AGE=$(( $(date +%s) - CACHE_MTIME ))\n23\t  if [ \"$AGE\" -lt 300 ]; then\n24\t    echo \"[$TS] harness-health-self-scan: CACHE-HIT age=${AGE}s session=$SID\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n25\t    exit 0\n26\t  fi\n27\tfi\n28\t\n29\t# === Aggregation counters ===\n30\tHIGH=0\n31\tMEDIUM=0\n32\tLOW=0\n33\tSKIP=0\n34\tFAIL_DETAILS=\"\"\n35\t\n36\temit_fail() {\n37\t  local signal=\"$1\" sev=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n38\t  case \"$sev\" in\n39\t    HIGH) HIGH=$((HIGH + 1)) ;;\n40\t    MEDIUM) MEDIUM=$((MEDIUM + 1)) ;;"}]},"uuid":"f9e4f9fa-3c38-4bc3-8622-fede15c5e3cd","timestamp":"2026-05-09T07:29:56.736Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\harness-health-self-scan.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# harness-health-self-scan.sh — continuous harness-health verification.\n# Implements 12-signal catalog HH-1..HH-12 codified in agent-workspace/{proposals,constitution}/harness-health-protocol.md\n# (currently at proposals/; mv to constitution/ pending M-S173-1 deny-lift workaround).\n# Triggers: UserPromptSubmit (every prompt; same-session caching) + SessionStart (each session entry).\n# Phase 3.5 T6 deliverable; D-035 ratified S173 via AskUserQuestion Q3=A.\n# <2s perf budget on UserPromptSubmit. Exit 0 ALWAYS (informational, not blocking).\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nHEALTH_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health.log\"\nCACHE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health-cache-${SID}\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\n# === Same-session cache check (5-min TTL) ===\nif [ -f \"$CACHE\" ]; then\n  CACHE_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  AGE=$(( $(date +%s) - CACHE_MTIME ))\n  if [ \"$AGE\" -lt 300 ]; then\n    echo \"[$TS] harness-health-self-scan: CACHE-HIT age=${AGE}s session=$SID\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 0\n  fi\nfi\n\n# === Aggregation counters ===\nHIGH=0\nMEDIUM=0\nLOW=0\nSKIP=0\nFAIL_DETAILS=\"\"\n\nemit_fail() {\n  local signal=\"$1\" sev=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n  case \"$sev\" in\n    HIGH) HIGH=$((HIGH + 1)) ;;\n    MEDIUM) MEDIUM=$((MEDIUM + 1)) ;;","numLines":40,"startLine":1,"totalLines":343}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b26b5d8d-32e4-4653-9e83-5460f21f1537","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d445a8b-bf27-47e3-8d5e-cf5f511500fa","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

Bundling them maximizes density per `qa_bundle_all_pending.md` mega-bundle doctrine.\n\n---\n\n## Q1 — T5 charter-tier: Approve harness-health-protocol.md NEW constitution authoring with 12-signal catalog?\n\n**Why-needed**: T5 is the constitution-file authoring step. Locks signal catalog (HH-1..HH-12) for T6 implementation. Per Phase 3.5 plan §T5.2, signals span Stop-firing / UserPromptSubmit-firing / promote-rule-cycle freshness / Auto-detect candidates without companion hook / Tier 1 bloat / dispatch-pending JSONL aging / checkpoint freshness / charter md5 stability / mistake-log freshness / firing-test coverage / .session-hooks.log liveness / project.md ↔ current-execution.md Phase coherence.\n\n**Proposed signal set (HH-1..HH-12) per Phase 3.5 plan §T5.2**:\n- HH-1: Stop hook ≥1 fire/session\n- HH-2: UserPromptSubmit ≥1 fire/non-trivial prompt\n- HH-3: Promote-rule cycle delta <8 sessions\n- HH-4: Auto-detect candidates without companion hook = 0\n- HH-5: Tier 1 bloat ≤8K tok\n- HH-6: dispatch-pending JSONL no `state=pending` >2hr\n- HH-7: Checkpoint freshness <2 sessions or <24hr\n- HH-8: Charter md5 unchanged in non-ratify session\n- HH-9: mistake-log.md last entry mtime within 5min of session-end\n- HH-10: Each priority-1 hook has companion firing-test\n- HH-11: .session-hooks.log mtime within session active duration\n- HH-12: current-execution.md Phase = project.md Phase\n\n- **A**: ✅ Approve as drafted (12 signals; binding contract for T6 hook) **(Recommended)** — signal set covers all 3 surfaced empirical-firing gaps (Stop hook 0 fires + promote-rule backlog + Auto-detect orphans) + adds defense-in-depth.\n- **B**: Approve BUT user provides amended outline first — user re-scopes signal catalog before agent authors.\n- **C**: Approve with reduced scope (8 signals; defer 4 to v1.1) — pick which 8 are highest priority.\n- **D**: Defer T5 to Phase 4+ (more empirical evidence needed; close Phase 3.5 with T1+T2+T3+T4+T7 only).\n\n---\n\n## Q2 — T8 charter-tier: Approve charter v1.0 → v1.1 with NEW Core Principle 11?\n\n**Proposed Principle 11 wording (verbatim, per Phase 3.5 plan §T8.1)**:\n\n> **Principle 11 — Harness must self-verify firing, not self-attest existence.** Every hook ships with a companion firing-test; T6 harness-health-self-scan continuously verifies signal-set HH-1..HH-N. Ritual closure of a track is forbidden until empirical-firing evidence is captured.\n\n**Why-needed**: Codifies the meta-anti-pattern surfaced in Phase 2.5 (8/8 ritual GREEN audit but Stop hook 0 fires + promote-rule 6+ session backlog + 20 Auto-detect candidates orphaned). Per Charter § Revision Protocol: \"A core principle proves materially wrong through 30+ hours of real use\" — Phase 2.5 → Phase 3.5 evidence chain (~60K main wasted across 22 ROUTINE-IDLE sessions S148-S170 + 3 user-pushed defect surfaces) qualifies as the \"30+ hours of real use\" evidence.\n\n**48hr cool-down**: Charter Revision Protocol mandates 48hr cool-down before committing change. S173 = proposal author; ratification ≥S174 + 48hr (= ~S180+).\n\n- **A**: ✅ Approve verbatim wording + version bump v1.0→v1.1 + 48hr cool-down honored **(Recommended)** — protocol-compliant; T8.3 charter file edit ≥S180.\n- **B**: Approve verbatim + EXPEDITE skip 48hr cool-down — small wording fix justification (not principle reversal); requires explicit user expedite override.\n- **C**: Approve principle but amend wording — user provides preferred phrasing (e.g., shorten / different framing / move to Anti-Charter section / etc).\n- **D**: Defer T8 to Phase 4+ — collect 30+ more days of harness-health-self-scan firing telemetry before charter promotion.\n\n---\n\n## Q3 — T6 charter-tier: Approve harness-health-self-scan.sh hook design?\n\n**Proposed design (per Phase 3.5 plan §T6.1-T6.5)**:\n- Trigger: UserPromptSubmit chain (every prompt) + SessionStart chain (each session entry)\n- Perf budget: <2s on UserPromptSubmit (must not bottleneck typing flow)\n- Output: aggregates FAILs across HH-1..HH-12 signals; emits via `<system-reminder>` channel (UserPromptSubmit) or stderr (SessionStart)\n- Optimization: cheap-first ordering (file mtime checks before grep on 1MB log); cache results across same-session prompts; suppress known-known issues (e.g., KI-S49b-1 Stop-hook Windows quirk)\n- Companion firing-test: synthesizes ≥1 deliberate FAIL state (e.g., touches Tier 1 to bloat past 8K) and asserts hook detects + reports\n\n**Why charter-tier (not IMPL-tier)**: hook fires on EVERY prompt; affects routine LLM context; charter implications (Tier 1 ceiling adjacent + system-reminder pollution risk).\n\n- **A**: ✅ Approve as designed **(Recommended)** — full UserPromptSubmit + SessionStart coverage; <2s perf budget; defense-in-depth for empirical-firing.\n- **B**: Approve with reduced scope (Stop-only hook, NOT UserPromptSubmit) — lower perf overhead; trade-off: misses mid-session FAILs.\n- **C**: Approve BUT defer authoring to S+1 — depends T5 ratify first; T6 needs T5 protocol locked.\n- **D**: Defer T6 entirely — author harness-health-self-scan only after Phase 4 empirical baseline.\n\n---\n\n## Q4 — Sequencing scope: After T5+T6+T8 ratified, what work order for S174+?\n\n**Why-needed**: Decides which residue/follow-on work runs FIRST after charter user-gate clears. SCOPE-tier (multi-track ordering decision affecting Phase 3 resume timing).\n\n- **A**: ✅ HH-G portability close FIRST **(Recommended)** — HH-G.1 general-harness CLAUDE.md template + HH-G.3 /attach smoke against fresh dir (`C:/htdocs/test-harness-portability/`); ~80-120K main; closes Phase 2.5 last 2 NOT-STARTED deliverables.\n- **B**: M-S171-1 prevention hooks FIRST — idle-escape-detector.sh + phase-status-coherence.sh; ~60-80K main; harness self-protection against repeat of 22-session ROUTINE-IDLE waste pattern (M-S171-1 documented; high-recurrence risk if not authored).\n- **C**: Phase 3 Track I Bayesian calibration FIRST — 008-S45 sub-plan; advances product roadmap (KOL calibration data); requires Phase 3.5 close validation first.\n- **D**: Phase 3 Tracks J+K BC-7 sentiment+pump FIRST — 009-S51 sub-plan (D-032 architecture ratified S65); largest scope sub-plan; PAUSED awaiting Phase 3.5 close.\n\n---\n\n## Answer-fill section (human writes inline)\n\n**Q1 answer**: ____ (A/B/C/D + optional notes)\n\n**Q2 answer**: ____ (A/B/C/D + optional notes)\n\n**Q3 answer**: ____ (A/B/C/D + optional notes)\n\n**Q4 answer**: ____ (A/B/C/D + optional notes)\n\n---\n\n## Auto-mv eligibility\n\nPer HH-E.2 (D-031) Auto-mv rule:\n- Status field MUST start with `answered-` / `closed-` / `resolved-` for hook to mv → answered/\n- No `wait_until:` future timestamp present\n- No `.auto-mv-paused` global kill switch\n- Hook `qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` performs the mv (not direct Bash)\n\nWhen all 4 answers received: human (or auto-mv hook based on frontmatter status update) moves bundle pending/ → answered/. Each rule earned through real experience (post-mortem, drift detection, user correction).\n4\t> Rules here have higher status than convention but lower than constitution.\n5\t\n6\t## Rule Format\n7\t\n8\tUse this template:\n9\t\n10\t```markdown\n11\t### YYYY-MM-DD: [Short rule name]\n12\t**Context**: What situation triggered learning this\n13\t**Rule**: The actionable rule\n14\t**Anti-example**: What was done wrong\n15\t**Correct example**: What should be done instead\n16\t**Severity**: critical | high | medium | low\n17\t**Auto-detect**: yes/no — can a drift signal catch this?\n18\t```\n19\t\n20\t---\n21\t\n22\t## Recent Rules (digest; last 5)\n23\t\n24\t### 2026-05-09 (S218): Hooks that write per-key state sidecars MUST clean up at session-end (or via periodic cleanup) — L-S218-1\n25\t**Context**: S218 audit during firing-test smoke-cache cleanup discovered 257 stale `.last-event-ts-${session}` files in `agent-workspace/memory/` written by `scripts/hooks/component-telemetry.sh:169` (one per CC session UUID). No cleanup logic; accumulating since component-telemetry hook landing. Same structural pattern as S218 turn-1's firing-test smoke-cache leak (1st instance, fixed via cleanup-trap extension). 2nd instance triggers AP-23 RED FLAG: \"2nd instance mandates promote-or-retire (not inline accumulation)\". Bulk-cleaned 243 files older than 24h via `find -mtime +0 -delete`; 14 fresh files (today's active sessions) retained. Disk space recovered minimal (~3.5 KB) but inode count recovered + ls/grep noise eliminated.\n26\t**Rule**: Any hook that writes per-key (per-session, per-dispatch, per-tool-call) state sidecar files MUST either: (a) clean its own sidecar at session-end via paired Stop-hook handler, OR (b) be paired with a periodic cleanup hook that scavenges sidecars older than configurable TTL (default 24h). Sidecar accumulation is the same anti-pattern class as L-S213-1 (per-session sidecars stuck-pending forever) — visible signal is \"ls dotfile count grows monotonically\".\n27\t**Anti-example**: `compute_duration_ms() { local ts_file=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.last-event-ts-${session}\"; ... printf '%s\\n' \"$now\" > \"$ts_file\"; }` writes per-session sidecar but no Stop-hook cleanup; 257 files accumulated.\n28\t**Correct example**: pair sidecar writer with Stop-hook handler `[ -f \"$MEMORY_DIR/.last-event-ts-${SID}\" ] && rm -f \"$MEMORY_DIR/.last-event-ts-${SID}\"`; OR cron-style sweeper `find $MEMORY_DIR -maxdepth 1 -name '.last-event-ts-*' -mtime +1 -delete` invoked weekly.\n29\t**Severity**: low (cosmetic + minor inode pollution; no functional impact). But pattern recurrence (firing-test smoke + component-telemetry per-session) signals systemic gap.\n30\t**Auto-detect**: candidate — Stop-hook check `find $MEMORY_DIR -maxdepth 1 -name '.*-cache-*' -o -name '.last-event-ts-*' -o -name '.dispatch-pending-*' | wc -l > 50 ⇒ WARN sidecar-accumulation`. ~10 LOC.\n31\t**Promotion target**: hook (auto-detect WARN) + structural fix to `scripts/hooks/component-telemetry.sh` (add Stop-hook handler section). Skill/charter NOT needed (low-blast-radius cleanup pattern).\n32\t**Related cross-boundary observation (S218 turn-4, 2026-05-09)**: same accumulation class observed in `human-workspace/notifications/` — 793 files since 2026-04-29 (535 warn-class, 152 incomplete, 87 ready, 1 urgent singleton). `scripts/hooks/tracking-retention.sh:46` defines NOTIF_DIR but only WRITES cap-breach notifications, no rotation. Cannot auto-clean per workspace contract (human-workspace = human-owned; agent write channel only). QUEUED for human decision: rotation policy needed (e.g., warn-class >7d → archive subdir; ready-class consumed-then-archive; urgent.md is already singleton-overwrite). Cross-reference for future portability.md / retention-policy.md charter consideration.\n33\t\n34\t**Promotion status (S218, 2026-05-09)**: FULL CHAIN LANDED within same session. (1) DATA-HYGIENE LANDED: 243 stale files removed via `find -mtime +0 -delete`. (2) STRUCTURAL FIX LANDED: `scripts/hooks/component-telemetry.sh:86-92` +7 LOC SessionStart-time sweeper (`find $MEMORY_DIR -maxdepth 1 -name '.last-event-ts-*' -mtime +1 -delete`) scoped to `HOOK_EVENT_NAME = SessionStart` only (PostToolUse / SubagentStop unaffected). Smoke-tested both positive (stale removed, fresh kept) + negative (PostToolUse does not sweep). (3) FIRING-TEST EXTENSION LANDED: `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/component-telemetry-fire-test.sh` +TC-K (sweeper positive) + TC-L (sweeper scope-negative); suite 12/12 PASS post-extension. Auto-detect WARN-hook (sidecar-accumulation >50 files) DEFERRED to next harness-health firing-test cycle (cheapest-by-RISK; structural fix prevents the accumulation pattern, auto-detect would catch if the structural fix is ever removed). Connection to L-S213-1: same sidecar-lifecycle-management class but lifecycle-completion (S213) vs lifecycle-cleanup-on-staleness (S218). Connection to L-S217-1: full chain (DATA-HYGIENE + STRUCTURAL + FIRING-TEST) within same session = fastest L-rule chain this phase.\n35\t\n36\t### 2026-05-09 (S217): HH-12 phase-coherence parse must accommodate project.md table-row format (no \"Phase\" prefix) — L-S217-1\n37\t**Context**: HH-12 emitted `SKIP=phase-parse-failed` continuously since hook landing (S173). Empirical pipeline trace at S217: `current-execution.md` row format `## S216 — Phase 3.5 — ...` parses to `ce_phase=\"Phase 3.5\"` ✓; `project.md` table-row format `| 3.5 — Harness Deepening ... | IN PROGRESS PARTIAL-S173 | ...` parses to `proj_phase=\"\"` ✗ because the regex `grep -oE 'Phase [0-9.]+'` expects literal \"Phase\" prefix that the table-row lacks. Re-fired identical 4-Q AskUserQuestion at S219 to recover the answer state (all-A re-confirmed, no semantic delta).\n**Rule**: Whenever an AskUserQuestion call answers an open Q&A bundle (askuserquestion_fired: true in frontmatter), the SAME turn MUST: (a) update bundle frontmatter `status:` to `answered-via-AskUserQuestion-YYYY-MM-DD-S<NNN>`, (b) inline answers in the answer-fill section, (c) record D-NNN refs back into frontmatter `related_decisions:`. Anti-pattern: firing AskUserQuestion + creating D-NNN but leaving the source bundle frontmatter open — auto-mv hook (HH-E.2 / D-031) never fires the mv, bundle accumulates, repeat sessions re-flag it as HUMAN-blocking.\n**Anti-example**: S173 fired 4-Q AskUserQuestion + created D-033/D-034/D-035 + left `status: pending` in source bundle → 46 sessions of false-positive HUMAN-blocking flag.\n**Correct example**: S219 close-the-loop pattern — same turn that fires AskUserQuestion writes back: frontmatter status, inline answers, D-NNN refs. Auto-mv hook then transitions pending → answered/ on next Stop.\n**Severity**: medium (chronic false-positive in priority-ordering signal; wastes ~500 tok/session in re-flag context; not catastrophic but exact-class of \"harness self-attests, doesn't self-verify firing\" Charter Principle 11 candidate addresses).\n**Auto-detect**: candidate — Stop-tier hook scan: for each `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/*.md`, parse `askuserquestion_fired: true` AND `status: pending` AND mtime>24h ⇒ WARN bundle-fired-but-not-closed. ~15 LOC. closes the loophole.\n59\t\n60\t---"}]},"uuid":"bbcf3a29-e87c-4491-9ef4-f497489a6f35","timestamp":"2026-05-09T07:35:28.012Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\proposals\\charter-revision-v1.1-harness-self-verify-firing.md","content":"# Charter Revision Proposal — v1.0 → v1.1: Add Principle 11 (Harness Self-Verify Firing)\n\n> **Status**: PROPOSED 2026-05-07 S173 (via AskUserQuestion S173 Q2=A user explicit approval)\n> **Cool-down active**: 48hr per `PROJECT_CHARTER.md` § Revision Protocol — earliest ratification S180+ (≥2026-05-09 12:00 ICT)\n> **Authority chain**: Phase 3.5 master plan §T8 → S173 charter user-gate → this file\n> **Companion artifacts**: `agent-workspace/proposals/harness-health-protocol.md` (T5; D-033) + `scripts/hooks/harness-health-self-scan.sh` (T6; D-035)\n\n---\n\n## § 1 — Written rationale (Charter Revision Protocol § \"Written rationale with evidence\")\n\n### Evidence chain\n\n**Phase 2.5 ritual closure → empirical failure → Phase 3.5 charter response**:\n\n| Date | Event | Evidence anchor |\n|---|---|---|\n| 2026-05-05 (S49a) | Phase 2.5 audit declared 8/8 GREEN ritual closure | `observations/2026-05-05-S49a-phase-2.5-audit-verdict.md` |\n| 2026-05-05 (S49b) | M-S49b-1 surfaced: `autonomous-stop-watchdog.sh` wired + smoke-test passes BUT 0 `Stop session=` entries in `.session-hooks.log` across ~10 turns | mistake-log.md M-S49b-1; session log S49b |\n| 2026-05-05 (S49b) | Promote-rule backlog surfaced: last cycle S43c (2026-05-04); 6+ sessions accumulated agent-notes without `promotion-cycle-trigger.sh` firing | observation file gap visible via Glob |\n| 2026-05-05 (S49b) | ~20 Auto-detect orphans surfaced: agent-notes entries tagged `Auto-detect: yes` with no companion shipped hook | manual grep audit |\n| 2026-05-05 (S50) | User directive: \"combine toàn bộ vào new session, dành nguyên phase mới để làm thật kĩ lưỡng mọi yêu cầu về harness cho chúng thực sự hoạt động\" | chat 2026-05-05 18:25 |\n| 2026-05-05 (S51-S79) | Phase 3.5 T1+T2+T3+T4+T7 SHIPPED: post-mortem + Stop→UserPromptSubmit migration + promote-rule backlog drain + L-S49b-4 charter promote + 63/63 firing-tests across HH-A..HH-H + L-S49b-4 backlog (15 → 0) | Phase 3.5 plan frontmatter |\n| 2026-05-06 (S148-S170) | 22 consecutive ROUTINE-IDLE sessions silently labeled \"Phase 3\" while project.md said \"Phase 2.5 IN PROGRESS / Phase 3 PAUSED\" — surface-4 of meta-anti-pattern: state-claim drift without empirical verification | M-S171-1 + S172 audit observation |\n| 2026-05-07 (S171) | User push: \"sao lại dừng rồi, không chạy autonomous nữa? lỗi harness à? update, note, fix, continue run\" — surfaced via user, NOT via harness self-detection | session log S171 |\n| 2026-05-07 (S172) | FOCUSED_AUDIT empirically verified Phase 2.5 + Phase 3 + Phase 3.5 status; reconciled project.md ↔ current-execution.md | `observations/2026-05-07-S172-phase-audit-reconciliation.md` |\n\n### \"30+ hours of real use\" qualification (Charter Revision Protocol gate)\n\n- Phase 2.5 ritual close S49a → Phase 3.5 entry S50 → S173 (≥123 sessions of harness real use)\n- Cumulative harness-firing telemetry: ~63/63 firing-tests + 22-session ROUTINE-IDLE sustained pattern + 4 user-push surfaces (M-S49b-1 / promote-rule / Auto-detect orphans / phase-claim drift)\n- Time elapsed S49a → S173: 2 days; sessions: 124 (≈8-12 hours/day Vietnamese-time autonomous loop = ~16-24 hours of real use)\n\n**Charter clause \"30+ hours of real use\" interpretation**: stockforge accelerated dogfood (autonomous loop + multi-session/day) compresses calendar time. By session count + empirical incident count, the qualification is satisfied. User explicit approval Q2=A confirms acceptance of this interpretation.\n\n### Why ELEVATE to charter (not hold at constitution-tier)\n\n- T5 codifies the **mechanism** (HH-1..HH-12 signal catalog) — constitution-tier\n- T8 codifies the **principle** (harness must self-verify, not self-attest) — charter-tier because:\n  - Affects ALL future hooks (every new hook must ship with firing-test per Phase 3.5 Hard Rule #2)\n  - Cross-cuts every track in every phase (not bounded to harness phase)\n  - Inverts the prior implicit principle (\"ritual closure suffices\") — substantive doctrine shift, not implementation detail\n  - Required to bind FUTURE phase-close audits (Phase 4+ would otherwise repeat Phase 2.5 anti-pattern)\n\n---\n\n## § 2 — Proposed Principle 11 wording (verbatim)\n\nThe following text is to be inserted into `PROJECT_CHARTER.md` § Core Principles, between current Principle 10 and § The Four-Tier Signal Architecture:\n\n> **11. Harness must self-verify firing, not self-attest existence** — Every hook ships with a companion firing-test in `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/`; the continuous `harness-health-self-scan` hook verifies signal-set HH-1 through HH-N (codified in `agent-workspace/constitution/harness-health-protocol.md`) on every UserPromptSubmit + SessionStart event. Ritual closure of a track (file existence + smoke-test exit 0) is forbidden until empirical-firing evidence (production log entry / artifact / telemetry row from real session activity) is captured.\n\n### Why this exact wording\n\n- \"Self-verify firing, not self-attest existence\" — the meta-anti-pattern crystallized in 6 words; mirrors AP-23 (LLM-Guardian) inversion.\n- \"Companion firing-test\" + \"harness-health-self-scan\" — names concrete artifacts that operationalize the principle (no hand-wave).\n- \"HH-1 through HH-N\" + reference to constitution file — extensible signal set without requiring future charter amendments for catalog growth.\n- \"Ritual closure forbidden until empirical-firing evidence captured\" — directly addresses the Phase 2.5 failure mode; closes the loophole.\n\n---","numLines":60,"startLine":1,"totalLines":183}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7f674716-27d3-46a7-80ad-85567f92f1f6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d445a8b-bf27-47e3-8d5e-cf5f511500fa","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"66530c98-49d6-4dfb-bfe4-acb7c96f5a5a","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"ea959916-7a29-4f7c-a00b-0b296143cb55","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01S5yqhbzavYGugiQ3hABDwP","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tstatus: PROPOSAL\n3\tproposal_id: L-S208-1\n4\tcreated_at: 2026-05-09\n5\tcreated_via: S213 main-session opus47-max (Phase 3.5 L-S208-1 charter-tier promotion)\n6\ttarget_file: agent-workspace/constitution/portability.md (or new section in invariants.md)\n7\tratification_path: USER-GATE via Cluster C charter Q&A bundle (pending; M-S173-1 deny holds)\n8\tcompanion_hooks_already_shipped:\n9\t  - scripts/hooks/settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh (Stop hook; auto-detect anti-pattern; 6/6 firing-test PASS at S212)\n10\tcompanion_skill_already_shipped:\n11\t  - .claude/commands/vbw-check.md § Verify-Settings.json-Hook-Portability subroutine (S213)\n12\trelated_lessons:\n13\t  - L-S11-1 (Windows portability — pairs with this rule as cross-platform doctrine)\n14\t  - L-S204-1 (verify-before-trust — applied 5x across S208-S212 chain)\n15\t---\n16\t\n17\t# Charter Amendment Proposal — Settings.json Hook Command Portability (L-S208-1)\n18\t\n19\t## Why this proposal exists\n20\t\n21\tThe S208-S212 root-cause investigation surfaced a catastrophic-class harness regression: 3 SessionStart/UserPromptSubmit hooks (`harness-health-self-scan`, `idle-escape-detector`, `phase-status-coherence`) were silently dormant in real-production for 2 days (2026-05-07 → 2026-05-09), undetected by the very harness-health emergency-broadcast signal (HH-1) that was supposed to detect such regressions — because HH-1 itself was the dormant hook.\n22\t\n23\tThe mechanism: `.claude/settings.json` hook entries used **bash-inline-env-var-prefix syntax** in their `\"command\":` field — e.g.,\n24\t`\"command\": \"CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/harness-health-self-scan.sh\\\"\"`.\n25\t\n26\tThis pattern works in a bash subshell (where firing-tests run, giving false confidence) but **silently fails in Claude Code's Windows hook runtime**: the outer process tokenizes the command string and treats `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart` as a separate command, raising `CommandNotFoundException` (verified via direct PowerShell repro) and never launching the hook.\n27\t\n28\t**Empirical evidence chain (S208-S212)**:\n29\t- S208: 5-fold cross-hook comparison — 3 hooks with inline-env-prefix have ZERO real-production emit lines since 2026-05-07; other hooks (no prefix) emit normally. PowerShell direct repro raises `CommandNotFoundException`. `bash -c '...'` wrapper repro succeeds.\n30\t- S209: env-dump diagnostic shipped (no-prefix vs with-prefix vs bashc-wrap variants).\n31\t- S210: diagnostic data confirmed `with-prefix` variant DOES NOT execute; `no-prefix` variant DOES; CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT is NOT natively exported by Claude Code on Windows (falsifies simplest \"drop the prefix\" fix).\n32\t- S211: bashc-wrap variant production-runtime confirmed (CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT correctly captured) → 6 production entries rewritten with `bash -c '...'` wrapper.\n33\t- S212: production verification at next UserPromptSubmit firing — all 3 previously-dormant hooks emitted `state=GREEN` lines; HH-1 cache mtime advanced from stuck-at-2026-05-07 to 2026-05-09. **Bug fully resolved + auto-detect Stop hook landed.**\n34\t\n35\tThis bug existed for 2 days because:\n36\t1. Firing-tests run in a bash subshell that DOES honor inline-env-prefix → false confidence.\n37\t2. HH-1 (the meta-detector for harness regressions) was itself a victim → no emergency broadcast.\n38\t3. No charter-tier rule prevented the anti-pattern at authoring time.\n39\t\n40\t## Proposed charter rule"}]},"uuid":"fffe78b7-0cf6-46e3-be84-5a72566352dd","timestamp":"2026-05-09T07:35:28.822Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\proposals\\charter-L-S208-1-settings-portability.md","content":"---\nstatus: PROPOSAL\nproposal_id: L-S208-1\ncreated_at: 2026-05-09\ncreated_via: S213 main-session opus47-max (Phase 3.5 L-S208-1 charter-tier promotion)\ntarget_file: agent-workspace/constitution/portability.md (or new section in invariants.md)\nratification_path: USER-GATE via Cluster C charter Q&A bundle (pending; M-S173-1 deny holds)\ncompanion_hooks_already_shipped:\n  - scripts/hooks/settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh (Stop hook; auto-detect anti-pattern; 6/6 firing-test PASS at S212)\ncompanion_skill_already_shipped:\n  - .claude/commands/vbw-check.md § Verify-Settings.json-Hook-Portability subroutine (S213)\nrelated_lessons:\n  - L-S11-1 (Windows portability — pairs with this rule as cross-platform doctrine)\n  - L-S204-1 (verify-before-trust — applied 5x across S208-S212 chain)\n---\n\n# Charter Amendment Proposal — Settings.json Hook Command Portability (L-S208-1)\n\n## Why this proposal exists\n\nThe S208-S212 root-cause investigation surfaced a catastrophic-class harness regression: 3 SessionStart/UserPromptSubmit hooks (`harness-health-self-scan`, `idle-escape-detector`, `phase-status-coherence`) were silently dormant in real-production for 2 days (2026-05-07 → 2026-05-09), undetected by the very harness-health emergency-broadcast signal (HH-1) that was supposed to detect such regressions — because HH-1 itself was the dormant hook.\n\nThe mechanism: `.claude/settings.json` hook entries used **bash-inline-env-var-prefix syntax** in their `\"command\":` field — e.g.,\n`\"command\": \"CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash \\\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/harness-health-self-scan.sh\\\"\"`.\n\nThis pattern works in a bash subshell (where firing-tests run, giving false confidence) but **silently fails in Claude Code's Windows hook runtime**: the outer process tokenizes the command string and treats `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart` as a separate command, raising `CommandNotFoundException` (verified via direct PowerShell repro) and never launching the hook.\n\n**Empirical evidence chain (S208-S212)**:\n- S208: 5-fold cross-hook comparison — 3 hooks with inline-env-prefix have ZERO real-production emit lines since 2026-05-07; other hooks (no prefix) emit normally. PowerShell direct repro raises `CommandNotFoundException`. `bash -c '...'` wrapper repro succeeds.\n- S209: env-dump diagnostic shipped (no-prefix vs with-prefix vs bashc-wrap variants).\n- S210: diagnostic data confirmed `with-prefix` variant DOES NOT execute; `no-prefix` variant DOES; CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT is NOT natively exported by Claude Code on Windows (falsifies simplest \"drop the prefix\" fix).\n- S211: bashc-wrap variant production-runtime confirmed (CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT correctly captured) → 6 production entries rewritten with `bash -c '...'` wrapper.\n- S212: production verification at next UserPromptSubmit firing — all 3 previously-dormant hooks emitted `state=GREEN` lines; HH-1 cache mtime advanced from stuck-at-2026-05-07 to 2026-05-09. **Bug fully resolved + auto-detect Stop hook landed.**\n\nThis bug existed for 2 days because:\n1. Firing-tests run in a bash subshell that DOES honor inline-env-prefix → false confidence.\n2. HH-1 (the meta-detector for harness regressions) was itself a victim → no emergency broadcast.\n3. No charter-tier rule prevented the anti-pattern at authoring time.\n\n## Proposed charter rule","numLines":40,"startLine":1,"totalLines":99}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"66530c98-49d6-4dfb-bfe4-acb7c96f5a5a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d445a8b-bf27-47e3-8d5e-cf5f511500fa","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"551b2dcb-8f7a-4c42-9435-0f3a0fe19c15","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01F7WQZX4vbxRfFjSpCuissj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0198NsHSrrkeioAeCed8NFwY","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\PROJECT_CHARTER.md","limit":80},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2830,"cache_read_input_tokens":112509,"output_tokens":408,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2830,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":408,"cache_read_input_tokens":112509,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2830,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2830},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CarcWQELTesRNTxfKViH3","type":"assistant","uuid":"803326d3-1356-4b2a-af12-25e62af08d1d","timestamp":"2026-05-09T07:37:11.940Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d445a8b-bf27-47e3-8d5e-cf5f511500fa","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"803326d3-1356-4b2a-af12-25e62af08d1d","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"ea959916-7a29-4f7c-a00b-0b296143cb55","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0198NsHSrrkeioAeCed8NFwY","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# PROJECT CHARTER\n2\t## StockForge — Personal Investment Advisor for Vietnamese Stock Market\n3\t\n4\t> **Status**: Immutable v1.0 — changes require explicit charter revision.\n5\t> **Scope of immutability**: Vision, principles, success criteria.\n6\t> **Things that do evolve**: Agent configs, specs, implementation, signal weights, eval sets.\n7\t\n8\t---\n9\t\n10\t## Vision\n11\t\n12\tAn AI-first investment research and advisory system that combines **quant fund methodology** with **Vietnam-specific edge sources** (KOL networks, crowd sentiment, pump pattern detection) to surface high-conviction opportunities in an inefficient market where logic-based screening alone is insufficient.\n13\t\n14\tPrimary user: project owner (self) + 3-5 trusted peers in Vietnamese investing community.\n15\t\n16\tPrimary goal: build a system that compounds edge over time through proprietary data accumulation, not through any single clever algorithm. After 12-24 months of dogfood, the system should be a genuine advisor I would pay money for if someone else built it.\n17\t\n18\tNot a goal: high-frequency trading, replacing human judgment, becoming a SaaS in Year 1, beating frontier LLMs at general reasoning, providing licensed financial advice.\n19\t\n20\t---\n21\t\n22\t## The Core Insight (the why)\n23\t\n24\t**Quant funds (Renaissance, Two Sigma, DE Shaw) win in efficient markets through clean data + execution speed + diverse alpha factors.** They don't use sentiment from Facebook/YouTube because at their scale it's noise, not signal — and in efficient markets it gets arbitraged away anyway.\n25\t\n26\t**Vietnam stock market inverts this.** It is structurally inefficient:\n27\t- 85-90% retail investor share (vs 20-25% in US)\n28\t- Information asymmetry between sophisticated and retail crowd\n29\t- KOL/influencer recommendations measurably move prices\n30\t- \"Đội lái\" (pump operators) operate openly\n31\t- News and narrative cycles dominate over fundamentals for months at a time\n32\t- Foreign flow creates non-fundamental price pressure\n33\t\n34\tIn this market, **sentiment + influence + narrative tracking is signal, not noise**. And LLMs can process Vietnamese unstructured text at scale that was impossible before 2023.\n35\t\n36\tThis creates a **structural arbitrage**: apply quant methodology + LLM reasoning + sentiment/influence data layers that no commercial product offers for VN. Position not as \"decision maker\" but as **advisor + alert watcher + bias mirror**.\n37\t\n38\t---\n39\t\n40\t## The Craft Philosophy\n41\t\n42\tTake the craft seriously. Build for personal use first, commercial second.\n43\t\n44\t- Production-grade engineering standards from Day 1 (DDD + clean architecture + multi-loop discipline)\n45\t- Commercial extension kept as option, never as driver\n46\t- When self-use conflicts with hypothetical-future-SaaS, self-use wins\n47\t- Quality bar: I genuinely trust output enough to act on real money decisions\n48\t- Edge comes from compounding moats (proprietary data, calibration, personal bias log), not from any single feature\n49\t\n50\tReference models: Renaissance Technologies (data + iteration discipline), Bridgewater (principles-based decision making), personal Bloomberg Terminal (data density + analysis depth), Athletic.com (subscription-quality content for niche audience).\n51\t\n52\t---\n53\t\n54\t## Core Principles (non-negotiable)\n55\t\n56\t1. **Evidence grounding** — every claim, every number traceable to source + as-of date. Hallucination in finance = bug, not stylistic issue.\n57\t\n58\t2. **Structured output over narrative** — multi-criteria assessment, never single \"buy/sell\" score. Output is trade-off matrix.\n59\t\n60\t3. **Adversarial by design** — bear + bull + critic + quant + behavior + manager perspectives. Never consensus from single agent.\n61\t\n62\t4. **Proprietary data moat** — every news ingested, every KOL recommendation tracked, every thesis post-mortem'd compounds the edge. Raw data is sacred.\n63\t\n64\t5. **Pattern transfer + local adaptation** — global setups (cyclical bottom, dividend value, GARP) + Vietnam-specific overlays (policy timing, foreign flow, narrative phase).\n65\t\n66\t6. **Human-in-loop is the product** — augment thinking, never replace it. System should challenge user's gut instinct without overriding it.\n67\t\n68\t7. **Dogfood mandatory** — if I don't use it weekly for real decisions, the feature gets killed. Self-use is the only validation that matters in Year 1.\n69\t\n70\t8. **Calibration over confidence** — system tracks its own accuracy across signal types, never claims confidence it hasn't earned through hit rate.\n71\t\n72\t9. **No LLM math** — LLM never generates numbers. All calculations through deterministic code with verified data inputs. LLM only interprets.\n73\t\n74\t10. **Position sizing & risk management are deterministic** — code-enforced rules, LLM cannot override. Maximum position size, sector concentration, stop loss rules are immutable per session.\n75\t\n76\t---\n77\t\n78\t## The Four-Tier Signal Architecture\n79\t\n80\tSystem operates on four distinct signal tiers, each with different lead time, reliability, and processing approach:"}]},"uuid":"efa20a4d-1236-493c-b9ca-5bfe614b1622","timestamp":"2026-05-09T07:37:12.505Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\PROJECT_CHARTER.md","content":"# PROJECT CHARTER\n## StockForge — Personal Investment Advisor for Vietnamese Stock Market\n\n> **Status**: Immutable v1.0 — changes require explicit charter revision.\n> **Scope of immutability**: Vision, principles, success criteria.\n> **Things that do evolve**: Agent configs, specs, implementation, signal weights, eval sets.\n\n---\n\n## Vision\n\nAn AI-first investment research and advisory system that combines **quant fund methodology** with **Vietnam-specific edge sources** (KOL networks, crowd sentiment, pump pattern detection) to surface high-conviction opportunities in an inefficient market where logic-based screening alone is insufficient.\n\nPrimary user: project owner (self) + 3-5 trusted peers in Vietnamese investing community.\n\nPrimary goal: build a system that compounds edge over time through proprietary data accumulation, not through any single clever algorithm. After 12-24 months of dogfood, the system should be a genuine advisor I would pay money for if someone else built it.\n\nNot a goal: high-frequency trading, replacing human judgment, becoming a SaaS in Year 1, beating frontier LLMs at general reasoning, providing licensed financial advice.\n\n---\n\n## The Core Insight (the why)\n\n**Quant funds (Renaissance, Two Sigma, DE Shaw) win in efficient markets through clean data + execution speed + diverse alpha factors.** They don't use sentiment from Facebook/YouTube because at their scale it's noise, not signal — and in efficient markets it gets arbitraged away anyway.\n\n**Vietnam stock market inverts this.** It is structurally inefficient:\n- 85-90% retail investor share (vs 20-25% in US)\n- Information asymmetry between sophisticated and retail crowd\n- KOL/influencer recommendations measurably move prices\n- \"Đội lái\" (pump operators) operate openly\n- News and narrative cycles dominate over fundamentals for months at a time\n- Foreign flow creates non-fundamental price pressure\n\nIn this market, **sentiment + influence + narrative tracking is signal, not noise**. And LLMs can process Vietnamese unstructured text at scale that was impossible before 2023.\n\nThis creates a **structural arbitrage**: apply quant methodology + LLM reasoning + sentiment/influence data layers that no commercial product offers for VN. Position not as \"decision maker\" but as **advisor + alert watcher + bias mirror**.\n\n---\n\n## The Craft Philosophy\n\nTake the craft seriously. Build for personal use first, commercial second.\n\n- Production-grade engineering standards from Day 1 (DDD + clean architecture + multi-loop discipline)\n- Commercial extension kept as option, never as driver\n- When self-use conflicts with hypothetical-future-SaaS, self-use wins\n- Quality bar: I genuinely trust output enough to act on real money decisions\n- Edge comes from compounding moats (proprietary data, calibration, personal bias log), not from any single feature\n\nReference models: Renaissance Technologies (data + iteration discipline), Bridgewater (principles-based decision making), personal Bloomberg Terminal (data density + analysis depth), Athletic.com (subscription-quality content for niche audience).\n\n---\n\n## Core Principles (non-negotiable)\n\n1. **Evidence grounding** — every claim, every number traceable to source + as-of date. Hallucination in finance = bug, not stylistic issue.\n\n2. **Structured output over narrative** — multi-criteria assessment, never single \"buy/sell\" score. Output is trade-off matrix.\n\n3. **Adversarial by design** — bear + bull + critic + quant + behavior + manager perspectives. Never consensus from single agent.\n\n4. **Proprietary data moat** — every news ingested, every KOL recommendation tracked, every thesis post-mortem'd compounds the edge. Raw data is sacred.\n\n5. **Pattern transfer + local adaptation** — global setups (cyclical bottom, dividend value, GARP) + Vietnam-specific overlays (policy timing, foreign flow, narrative phase).\n\n6. **Human-in-loop is the product** — augment thinking, never replace it. System should challenge user's gut instinct without overriding it.\n\n7. **Dogfood mandatory** — if I don't use it weekly for real decisions, the feature gets killed. Self-use is the only validation that matters in Year 1.\n\n8. **Calibration over confidence** — system tracks its own accuracy across signal types, never claims confidence it hasn't earned through hit rate.\n\n9. **No LLM math** — LLM never generates numbers. All calculations through deterministic code with verified data inputs. LLM only interprets.\n\n10. **Position sizing & risk management are deterministic** — code-enforced rules, LLM cannot override. Maximum position size, sector concentration, stop loss rules are immutable per session.\n\n---\n\n## The Four-Tier Signal Architecture\n\nSystem operates on four distinct signal tiers, each with different lead time, reliability, and processing approach:","numLines":80,"startLine":1,"totalLines":257}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"803326d3-1356-4b2a-af12-25e62af08d1d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d445a8b-bf27-47e3-8d5e-cf5f511500fa","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
