MAJOR version bump (v2.0) reserved for principle deletion/inversion.\n\n---\n\n## § 4 — 48-hour cool-down acknowledgment\n\nPer `PROJECT_CHARTER.md` § Revision Protocol:\n\n> Charter revisions require:\n> - Written rationale with evidence (linked to specific sessions/post-mortems)\n> - 48-hour cool-down before committing change\n> - Explicit version bump (v1.0 → v2.0)\n\n| Step | Status | Date |\n|---|---|---|\n| Written rationale with evidence | ✅ DONE | 2026-05-07 S173 (this proposal § 1) |\n| Source artifacts linked | ✅ DONE | M-S49b-1 mistake-log + S172 audit observation + Phase 3.5 plan + 22-session ROUTINE-IDLE pattern |\n| 48-hour cool-down period | 🟡 IN PROGRESS | Started 2026-05-07 ~05:30 ICT; earliest charter edit 2026-05-09 ~05:30 ICT |\n| Explicit version bump | ✅ PRE-AUTHORIZED | v1.0 → v1.1 (per Q2=A user approval) |\n\n**Cool-down monitoring**: Stop hook `auto-reboot-handoff-verify.sh` + `pre-checkpoint-close-verifier.sh` should NOT permit charter edit until 2026-05-09. Future session at S180+ may execute charter edit IFF this proposal still PROPOSED + cool-down elapsed + no SUPERSEDE event in interim.\n\n---\n\n## § 5 — Charter edit plan (post-cool-down)\n\n### Step 1 (S180+ session, ≥2026-05-09)\n\n```bash\n# Verify cool-down elapsed\nPROPOSAL_DATE=\"2026-05-07\"\nTODAY=$(date +%Y-%m-%d)\nDAYS_ELAPSED=$(python3 -c \"from datetime import date; print((date.fromisoformat('$TODAY') - date.fromisoformat('$PROPOSAL_DATE')).days)\")\n[ \"$DAYS_ELAPSED\" -ge 2 ] || { echo \"Cool-down active; abort\"; exit 1; }\n```\n\n### Step 2 (deny-lift via Bash mv pattern)\n\nPROJECT_CHARTER.md is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list (Edit + Write). Symmetric with T5 mv mechanism; auditable via git diff; no permission prompt latency.\n\n### Step 3 (set ratify marker for HH-8 charter-md5 signal)\n\nBefore edit:\n```bash\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID}\"\ntouch \"agent-workspace/memory/.charter-ratify-active-${SID}\"\n```\n\nThis marker authorizes HH-8 signal to PASS despite md5 change. 6 hooks rewritten with bash -c wrapper; HH-1 cache mtime advanced from stuck-at-2026-05-07 to 2026-05-09)\n  - HOOK-TIER auto-detect LANDED S213 (settings-inline-env-prefix-detector.sh + 6/6 firing-test PASS)\n  - SKILL-TIER LANDED S213 (vbw-check.md § Verify-Settings.json-Hook-Portability subroutine)\n  - CHARTER-TIER proposal authored S213 in proposals/charter-L-S208-1-settings-portability.md\n  - S220 deny-lift mode A approved (bundled with D-047 charter v1.1 + D-048 T5 protocol)\nrelated_decisions:\n  - D-038 (HH-C2 staleness watchdog — observed similar harness-portability gap class)\n  - L-S214-1 promotion chain (canonical .session-hooks.log path — companion harness-portability rule)\nrelated_lessons:\n  - L-S11-1 Windows portability (umbrella; 7220 bytes)\n---\n\n## Why ratified\n\nL-S208-1 root-cause investigation surfaced a catastrophic-class harness regression: 3 SessionStart/UserPromptSubmit hooks silently dormant in real-production for 2 days because `.claude/settings.json` hook entries used bash-inline-env-var-prefix syntax that fails on Claude Code's Windows hook runtime (bash subshell tokenizes `CLAUDE_HOOK_EVENT=SessionStart bash ...` as separate command). HH-1 itself was a victim — the meta-detector for harness regressions could not detect its own dormancy. Bug-fix + hook-tier auto-detect + skill-tier subroutine all LANDED S212-S213; charter-tier proposal pending in proposals/ until S220.\n\nS220 deny-lift cycle bundled this charter promotion with D-047 (Principle 11 ratification) + D-048 (T5 protocol promotion) per L-S43f-1/D-026 precedent (single permission ask = lower churn).\n\n## Mechanism (S220 turn)\n\n- `mv agent-workspace/proposals/charter-L-S208-1-settings-portability.md agent-workspace/constitution/portability.md` — file was untracked in git so plain mv used (will appear as new file in next commit per user's commit decision).\n- File content UNCHANGED post-mv.\n- `.claude/settings.json` deny block temp-lifted via `_S220_TEMP_LIFTED_` prefix per D-047 mechanism; Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n4\tallowed-tools: [Read, Glob, Grep, Bash, Write]\n5\t---\n6\t\n7\t# Skill: /attach\n8\t\n9\t## Purpose\n10\t\n11\tSingle-command harness port: copy `.claude/skills + agents + commands + hooks` (harness layer) plus `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` + `human-workspace/CLAUDE.md` skeletons + manifest.yaml from this project to a target directory. Stockforge biz layer (5 tagged skills + 5 hooks + biz docs) is excluded by default.\n12\t\n13\t## When to Use\n14\t\n15\t- User wants to start a fresh Claude Code project reusing this harness\n16\t- Sanity-check layer separation (run `--dry-run`; verify excluded paths)\n17\t- Peer asks for the harness; copy to their machine + git init\n18\t\n19\t## When NOT to Use\n20\t\n21\t- Internal restructure of this project — use direct file ops\n22\t- Anything that crosses project boundaries (don't `--attach` your own home dir)\n23\t- Phase 0 not yet complete — harness skeleton may still be churning; wait until Track 7 ships constitution port\n24\t\n25\t## Inputs\n26\t\n27\t| Arg | Required | Purpose |\n28\t|---|---|---|\n29\t| `<target-path>` | yes | Absolute or relative path to target project root |\n30\t| `--dry-run` | no | Print plan as table; copy nothing |\n31\t| `--include-personal` | no | Also copy `.claude/personal/` + `.env` |\n32\t| `--include-stockforge` | no | Also copy stockforge-tagged skills + biz docs (dev-mirror use case) |\n33\t| `--force` | no | Overwrite existing files at target without prompt |\n34\t\n35\t## Process\n36\t\n37\t1. **Read source manifest** — `Read .claude/manifest.yaml` from the current project. Parse layer tags + attach.default_includes/excludes.\n38\t2. **Validate target** — Bash check: target dir exists OR can mkdir parent. If target/CLAUDE.md exists AND no `--force`: abort with clear message.\n39\t3. **Build copy plan**:\n40\t   - INCLUDE: every path under `harness.skills`, `harness.agents`, `harness.commands`, `harness.hooks`, `harness.docs`\n41\t   - INCLUDE (hybrid): `hybrid.hooks` paths — copy + stub out `stockforge_part` lines\n42\t   - SKIP: every path under `stockforge.skills`, `stockforge.hooks`, `stockforge.docs` (unless `--include-stockforge`)\n43\t   - SKIP: every path under `personal.paths` (unless `--include-personal`)\n44\t   - SKIP: workspace state (`agent-workspace/memory/{decisions,sessions,checkpoints,thesis-log,calibration}`, `human-workspace/{user_prompt,decisions,q-and-a,notifications}`, `agent-workspace/raw-sessions`)\n45\t4. **Display plan** — always, even non-dry-run. Format: table of (action / path / size / reason).\n46\t5. **Stop if `--dry-run`** — exit cleanly after plan display.\n47\t6. **Execute copy** — see `references/procedures.md § Copy Steps` for bash one-liners (cp -r preserving structure; sed-replace env vars; hybrid stub).\n48\t7. **Generate skeleton at target**:\n49\t   - `CLAUDE.md` — minimal pointer to harness baseline + project-specific stub (template: `references/skeleton-templates.md`)\n50\t   - `.claude/manifest.yaml` — copy of source manifest with stockforge layer cleared (target re-categorizes own biz)\n51\t   - `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` — skeleton routing source\n52\t   - Empty skeleton dirs: `agent-workspace/memory/{sessions,decisions,checkpoints,observations}`, `human-workspace/{user_prompt,decisions,q-and-a/{pending,answered,stale},notifications}`\n53\t8. **Print success summary** — file count copied + skipped + skeleton paths created.\n54\t9. **Suggest next steps** — `cd <target> && git init && claude` then run `/session-start`.\n55\t\n56\t## Validation Pre-Conditions\n57\t\n58\t- Source manifest validates against drift V1-V7 (run `drift-signals-D1-D9.sh` first if uncertain)\n59\t- Source `.claude/skills/attach/SKILL.md` is THIS file (skill exists; trivially true)\n60\t- Target path is not source path or any ancestor (don't recurse into self)\n61\t\n62\t## Skeleton Output\n63\t\n64\tTarget post-/attach has working harness without biz logic. User runs `/session-start` and gets routing scaffold; runs `/session-end` and gets sessions log; etc. All harness commands functional.\n65\t\n66\tWhat target does NOT get: stockforge biz skills, charter, specs, eval-sets, obsidian vault, accumulated workspace state. Those are the user's own to author for their domain.\n67\t\n68\t## Anti-Patterns\n69\t\n70\t- Never copy `.git/` — target inits its own\n71\t- Never copy node_modules / .pytest_cache / __pycache__ — bloat\n72\t- Never overwrite target/CLAUDE.md silently — `--force` is explicit\n73\t- Never copy across project boundaries that haven't accepted the harness contract — get confirmation first\n74\t- Don't copy hybrid hook with stockforge_part code intact — must stub or remove (target may not have stockforge env vars)\n75\t\n76\t## Smoke Test (procedure for first /attach run)\n77\t\n78\t1. Pick scratch target: `/tmp/attach-smoke` (Linux) or `$env:TEMP\\attach-smoke` (Windows)\n79\t2. Run `--dry-run` — verify plan excludes 5 stockforge skills + 5 stockforge hooks\n80\t3. Run actual copy — verify file count matches plan\n81\t4. cd target + `claude` + `/session-start` — verify routing skeleton works\n82\t5. Diff target/.claude/skills/ vs source — should differ only in stockforge biz skills (5 absent)\n83\t6. Cleanup: `rm -rf <scratch>` (manual; skill never destroys)\n84\t\n85\t### Layer-separation assertion smoke (S48l HH-G.3 codified)\n86\t\n87\tStandalone Python harness that DOES NOT create files; validates manifest-level layer separation. Use for portability validation when actual copy not desired (autonomous loops avoid Skill tool prompts per UP-05).\n88\t\n89\t```bash\n90\tPYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8 python -c \"\n91\timport yaml\n92\tm = yaml.safe_load(open('.claude/manifest.yaml'))\n93\tinclude, exclude, hybrid = [], [], []\n94\tfor cat in ['skills','agents','commands','hooks','docs']:\n95\t    include += [e['path'] for e in (m.get('harness',{}).get(cat) or [])]\n96\t    exclude += [e['path'] for e in (m.get('stockforge',{}).get(cat) or [])]\n97\thybrid += [e['path'] for e in (m.get('hybrid',{}).get('hooks') or [])]\n98\tdefault_includes = next((v.get('default_includes') for v in m.values() if isinstance(v,dict) and 'default_includes' in v), [])\n99\t\n100\tassertions = [\n101\t    ('A1', 'agent-workspace/constitution/invariants-stockforge.md' in exclude),\n102\t    ('A2', 'agent-workspace/constitution/invariants.md' in default_includes),\n103\t    ('A3', 'agent-workspace/constitution/invariants-stockforge.md' not in default_includes),\n104\t    ('A4', sum(1 for _ in (m.get('harness',{}).get('skills') or [])) >= 17),\n105\t    ('A5', sum(1 for _ in (m.get('stockforge',{}).get('skills') or [])) == 5),\n106\t    ('A6', any('drift-signals' in p for p in hybrid)),\n107\t    ('A7', 'PROJECT_CHARTER.md' in exclude),\n108\t]\n109\terrors = [c for c, ok in assertions if not ok]\n110\tprint('GREEN' if not errors else 'RED ' + ','.join(errors))\n111\t\"\n112\t```\n113\t\n114\tExpected output: `GREEN`. If `RED <ids>`, manifest layer-tagging regressed — investigate before allowing /attach to consumers.\n115\t\n116\t## Constitution Layer Boundary (post-S48l HH-G.2)\n117\t\n118\tConstitution files split into harness vs stockforge per portability validation:\n119\t\n120\t| File | Layer | Notes |\n121\t|---|---|---|\n122\t| `architecture.md` | harness | DDD + clean architecture patterns; project-agnostic |\n123\t| `autonomous-protocol.md` | harness | Mode-A/B/C/D/E discipline + Rule 10 |\n124\t| `boundaries.md` | harness | What agent cannot do without human approval |\n125\t| `coding-principles.md` | harness | Karpathy + DDD applied; some I-S* refs but pattern-portable |\n126\t| `decision-discipline.md` | harness | Tier classification + grill discipline |\n127\t| `drift-signals.md` | harness | DR1-DR12 signals + Tiered Coverage Map |\n128\t| `financial-data-protocol.md` | harness (flagged `[needs-refactor]`) | Stock-domain content; portability split target post-HH-G |\n129\t| `invariants.md` | harness | General invariants I-1..I-52 only (post-S48l split) |\n130\t| `invariants-stockforge.md` | **stockforge** | Stock-domain I-S* invariants — NOT copied by /attach |\n131\t| `karpathy-principles.md` | harness | P1-P4 four-principles doctrine |\n132\t| `memory-routing-tree.md` | harness | 12-field routing schema |\n133\t| `memory-tiers.md` | harness | Tier 1/2/3 always-loaded vs JIT vs explicit-pull |\n134\t| `session-budgets.md` | harness | Type selection + budget bands |\n135\t| `vbw-protocol.md` | harness | Verify-Before-Write checkpoints |\n136\t\n137\tWhen porting to non-stock projects:\n138\t- Target gets 13 harness constitution files (general patterns)\n139\t- `invariants-stockforge.md` is excluded — target authors own invariants per their domain\n140\t- `financial-data-protocol.md` is currently included but flagged for further split if portability validation fails on a non-finance target\n141\t\n142\t## See Also\n143\t\n144\t- `.claude/manifest.yaml` — single source of truth for layer membership\n145\t- `references/procedures.md` — bash one-liners + edge cases\n146\t- `references/skeleton-templates.md` — skeleton file content\n147\t- D-003 REV-2 — tag-only realization (no `.claude/stockforge/` subtree)\n148\t- `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` § Subdirectories — what target needs to have empty\n149\t"}]},"uuid":"92293ae5-bb9d-47fe-90b8-d9dfbfa97878","timestamp":"2026-05-09T07:44:42.031Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\.claude\\skills\\attach\\SKILL.md","content":"---\nname: attach\ndescription: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. {"parentUuid":"f7c173c4-d3b5-4449-8b65-74a1f96ceb53","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"5a95da85-8749-4457-9326-1e3c9f695755","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01D8LEcB5oVysh7UKkinW4LM","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# /attach — Skeleton Templates\n2\t\n3\t> Templates for skeleton files generated at target project root.\n4\t> The skill procedure extracts sections from this file and writes them.\n5\t\n6\t## CLAUDE.md\n7\t\n8\t```markdown\n9\t# <PROJECT_NAME> — Claude Code Instructions\n10\t\n11\t> Generated by /attach from harness baseline. Customize freely. Target: <2.5K tokens portable.\n12\t\n13\t## Identity\n14\t\n15\tYou are **Claude Code**, the engineering partner for <PROJECT_NAME>.\n16\t\n17\t**Primary user**: <USER_NAME>.\n18\t**Stack**: <STACK_HERE>.\n19\t\n20\t---\n21\t\n22\t## Core Principles (Karpathy 4)\n23\t\n24\t**P1. Think Before Coding.** State assumptions. Surface tradeoffs. Push back when simpler approach exists. Stop when confused.\n25\t\n26\t**P2. Simplicity First.** Minimum code that solves the problem. No speculative features. If 200 lines could be 50, rewrite.\n27\t\n28\t**P3. Surgical Changes.** Touch only what the task requires. Match existing style. Every changed line traces to the task.\n29\t\n30\t**P4. Goal-Driven Execution.** Transform imperative → verifiable goals with concrete success criteria.\n31\t\n32\t---\n33\t\n34\t## Sandwich Pattern (Architect → Dev → Verifier)\n35\t\n36\tThree-persona discipline. Each session adopts ONE persona; never mix.\n37\t\n38\t| Persona | Subagent | Responsibility | Anti-pattern |\n39\t|---|---|---|---|\n40\t| **Architect** | sandwich-architect | Design + plan; produces session plan + ADR provenance. Does NOT write production code. | Mix PLAN + IMPL in same session (Session 4 catastrophic failure mode). |\n41\t| **Dev** | sandwich-dev | Execute per existing plan. Does NOT re-plan. | Refactor adjacent unrelated code (violates P3). |\n42\t| **Verifier** | sandwich-verifier | Adversarial review of Dev output. Fresh context — separate agent from author. | Same-agent self-review (echo chamber AP-1). |\n43\t\n44\t---\n45\t\n46\t## Session Protocol\n47\t\n48\t### Start\n49\t1. Read `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` → resolve active track\n50\t2. Read `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` → project state (when authored)\n51\t3. Read `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md` if recent\n52\t4. Read last 3 files in `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/` → recent context\n53\t5. Read `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` + `mistake-log.md` (pre-flight failure catalog)\n54\t6. Run VBW protocol before writing any spec/test/code\n55\t\n56\t### End\n57\t1. Update `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` (if architectural decisions made)\n58\t2. Write `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`\n59\t3. Update `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md`\n60\t4. If learned rule emerged → append to `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md`\n61\t5. Update `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` with new entries OR explicit \"no mistakes this session\" attestation\n62\t\n63\t---\n64\t\n65\t## Memory Tiering\n66\t\n67\tThree tiers govern what's loaded when:\n68\t\n69\t- **Tier 1 — Always-loaded** (CLAUDE.md + agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md + current-execution.md). Keep <8K tokens combined per session-budget enforcement.\n70\t- **Tier 2 — Just-in-time** (constitution files + skills + agents loaded when task demands). Read on relevance, not on session-start.\n71\t- **Tier 3 — Explicit-pull** (post-mortems + thesis logs + raw sessions). Loaded only when explicitly referenced or grep-targeted.\n72\t\n73\tAnti-pattern: loading entire codebase before starting = Session 4 catastrophic failure. Customize freely. Target: <2.5K tokens portable.\n\n## Identity\n\nYou are **Claude Code**, the engineering partner for <PROJECT_NAME>.\n\n**Primary user**: <USER_NAME>.\n**Stack**: <STACK_HERE>.\n\n---\n\n## Core Principles (Karpathy 4)\n\n**P1. Think Before Coding.** State assumptions. Surface tradeoffs. Push back when simpler approach exists. Stop when confused.\n\n**P2. Simplicity First.** Minimum code that solves the problem. No speculative features. If 200 lines could be 50, rewrite.\n\n**P3. Surgical Changes.** Touch only what the task requires. Match existing style. Every changed line traces to the task.\n\n**P4. Goal-Driven Execution.** Transform imperative → verifiable goals with concrete success criteria.\n\n---\n\n## Sandwich Pattern (Architect → Dev → Verifier)\n\nThree-persona discipline. Each session adopts ONE persona; never mix.\n\n| Persona | Subagent | Responsibility | Anti-pattern |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| **Architect** | sandwich-architect | Design + plan; produces session plan + ADR provenance. Does NOT write production code. | Mix PLAN + IMPL in same session (Session 4 catastrophic failure mode). |\n| **Dev** | sandwich-dev | Execute per existing plan. Does NOT re-plan. | Refactor adjacent unrelated code (violates P3). |\n| **Verifier** | sandwich-verifier | Adversarial review of Dev output. Fresh context — separate agent from author. | Same-agent self-review (echo chamber AP-1). |\n\n---\n\n## Session Protocol\n\n### Start\n1. Read `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` → resolve active track\n2. Read `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` → project state (when authored)\n3. Read `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md` if recent\n4. Read last 3 files in `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/` → recent context\n5. Read `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` + `mistake-log.md` (pre-flight failure catalog)\n6. Run VBW protocol before writing any spec/test/code\n\n### End\n1. Update `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` (if architectural decisions made)\n2. Write `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`\n3. Update `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md`\n4. If learned rule emerged → append to `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md`\n5. Update `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` with new entries OR explicit \"no mistakes this session\" attestation\n\n---\n\n## Memory Tiering\n\nThree tiers govern what's loaded when:\n\n- **Tier 1 — Always-loaded** (CLAUDE.md + agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md + current-execution.md). Keep <8K tokens combined per session-budget enforcement.\n- **Tier 2 — Just-in-time** (constitution files + skills + agents loaded when task demands). Read on relevance, not on session-start.\n- **Tier 3 — Explicit-pull** (post-mortems + thesis logs + raw sessions). Loaded only when explicitly referenced or grep-targeted.\n\nAnti-pattern: loading entire codebase before starting = Session 4 catastrophic failure. Use just-in-time loading.\n\n---\n\n## Q&A Escalation Doctrine (UP-06 NO-Silent-Default)\n\nWhen agent has a question worth asking, lifecycle is:\n","numLines":80,"startLine":1,"totalLines":240}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f7c173c4-d3b5-4449-8b65-74a1f96ceb53","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d445a8b-bf27-47e3-8d5e-cf5f511500fa","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

Goal-Driven Execution.** Transform imperative → verifiable goals with concrete success criteria.\n\n---\n\n## Harness Self-Verify Discipline (Charter Principle 11)\n\n**Every hook ships with a companion firing-test in `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/`.** The continuous `harness-health-self-scan` hook verifies signal-set HH-1 through HH-N (codified in `agent-workspace/constitution/harness-health-protocol.md`) on every UserPromptSubmit + SessionStart event. Goal-Driven Execution.** Transform imperative → verifiable goals with concrete success criteria.\n\n---\n\n## Sandwich Pattern (Architect → Dev → Verifier)","newString":"**P4. Goal-Driven Execution.** Transform imperative → verifiable goals with concrete success criteria.\n\n---\n\n## Harness Self-Verify Discipline (Charter Principle 11)\n\n**Every hook ships with a companion firing-test in `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/`.** The continuous `harness-health-self-scan` hook verifies signal-set HH-1 through HH-N (codified in `agent-workspace/constitution/harness-health-protocol.md`) on every UserPromptSubmit + SessionStart event. Ritual closure of a track (file existence + smoke-test exit 0) is forbidden until empirical-firing evidence (production log entry / artifact / telemetry row from real session activity) is captured.\n\nThis is the inversion of the \"ritual GREEN audit\" anti-pattern: existence + unit-smoke do NOT prove the hook fires in production. Only a real `.session-hooks.log` entry from a real session does.\n\n---\n\n## Sandwich Pattern (Architect → Dev → Verifier)","originalFile":"# /attach — Skeleton Templates\n\n> Templates for skeleton files generated at target project root.\n> The skill procedure extracts sections from this file and writes them.\n\n## CLAUDE.md\n\n```markdown\n# <PROJECT_NAME> — Claude Code Instructions\n\n> Generated by /attach from harness baseline. Customize freely. Target: <2.5K tokens portable.\n\n## Identity\n\nYou are **Claude Code**, the engineering partner for <PROJECT_NAME>.\n\n**Primary user**: <USER_NAME>.\n**Stack**: <STACK_HERE>.\n\n---\n\n## Core Principles (Karpathy 4)\n\n**P1. Think Before Coding.** State assumptions. Surface tradeoffs. Push back when simpler approach exists. Stop when confused.\n\n**P2. Simplicity First.** Minimum code that solves the problem. No speculative features. If 200 lines could be 50, rewrite.\n\n**P3. Surgical Changes.** Touch only what the task requires. Match existing style. Every changed line traces to the task.\n\n**P4. Goal-Driven Execution.** Transform imperative → verifiable goals with concrete success criteria.\n\n---\n\n## Sandwich Pattern (Architect → Dev → Verifier)\n\nThree-persona discipline. Each session adopts ONE persona; never mix.\n\n| Persona | Subagent | Responsibility | Anti-pattern |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| **Architect** | sandwich-architect | Design + plan; produces session plan + ADR provenance. Does NOT write production code. | Mix PLAN + IMPL in same session (Session 4 catastrophic failure mode). |\n| **Dev** | sandwich-dev | Execute per existing plan. Does NOT re-plan. | Refactor adjacent unrelated code (violates P3). |\n| **Verifier** | sandwich-verifier | Adversarial review of Dev output. Fresh context — separate agent from author. | Same-agent self-review (echo chamber AP-1). |\n\n---\n\n## Session Protocol\n\n### Start\n1. Read `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` → resolve active track\n2. Read `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` → project state (when authored)\n3. Read `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md` if recent\n4. Read last 3 files in `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/` → recent context\n5. Read `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` + `mistake-log.md` (pre-flight failure catalog)\n6. Run VBW protocol before writing any spec/test/code\n\n### End\n1. Update `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` (if architectural decisions made)\n2. Write `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`\n3. Update `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md`\n4. If learned rule emerged → append to `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md`\n5. Update `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` with new entries OR explicit \"no mistakes this session\" attestation\n\n---\n\n## Memory Tiering\n\nThree tiers govern what's loaded when:\n\n- **Tier 1 — Always-loaded** (CLAUDE.md + agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md + current-execution.md). Keep <8K tokens combined per session-budget enforcement.\n- **Tier 2 — Just-in-time** (constitution files + skills + agents loaded when task demands). Read on relevance, not on session-start.\n- **Tier 3 — Explicit-pull** (post-mortems + thesis logs + raw sessions). Loaded only when explicitly referenced or grep-targeted.\n\nAnti-pattern: loading entire codebase before starting = Session 4 catastrophic failure. Use just-in-time loading.\n\n---\n\n## Q&A Escalation Doctrine (UP-06 NO-Silent-Default)\n\nWhen agent has a question worth asking, lifecycle is:\n\n1. Compose Q&A bundle file in `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/<YYYY-MM-DD>-NNN-<slug>.md`\n2. Frontmatter `status:` field reflects state (`pending`, `answered-via-chat`, `answered-via-AskUserQuestion`, `closed-*`, `resolved-*`)\n3. For SCOPE/CHARTER-tier questions: fire `AskUserQuestion` tool (≤4 questions per call); record outcome in bundle\n4. Agent NEVER silently defaults on unanswered questions; either fire AskUserQuestion OR keep pending in file\n5. Stale bundles >48h trigger URGENT notification via `qa-stale-urgent-escalator.sh` Stop hook\n\nAuto-mv: bundles with `status:` matching `^(answered-|closed-|resolved-)` are auto-moved `pending/` → `answered/` by `qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` Stop hook (override via `wait_until:` ISO timestamp OR global `.auto-mv-paused` kill switch).\n\n---\n\n## Hard Rules\n\n- **Domain layer has zero framework dependency.** Pure Python in `packages/domain/` (or equivalent stack-appropriate location). No FastAPI, no Pydantic, no ORM in domain.\n- **Cross-BC communication via contracts only.** Bounded contexts never directly import from each other's domain/application packages.\n- **VBW Protocol mandatory before writing specs/tests/code.** Read actual source, not memory/convention.\n- **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** Files in `agent-workspace/constitution/` require deny-lift cycle for edits.\n- **Agents MUST NOT `git commit` unless user explicitly requests.** Stage changes, report, let user decide.\n- **User prompt overrides ALL defaults.** If user says \"skip X\", that trumps any skill/workflow.\n- **Context-threshold band**: 180K wind-down (auto-prep handoff) / 220K cliff (auto-reboot via `session-self-reboot.sh`) / 250K hard_cap (mandatory split).\n- **Deterministic gates** (mypy, pytest, ruff equivalents) must pass before commit. Max 3 retry before escalate.\n\n---\n\n## Session Types\n\n| Type | Budget | Purpose |\n|---|---|---|\n| PLAN | 50-80K | Architect, produces session plan |\n| FOCUSED IMPL | 100-150K | Dev, 1-3 tasks from plan |\n| MULTI-TASK IMPL | 150-250K | Dev, 4-10 tasks from plan |\n| VERIFY | 30-60K | Verifier, adversarial review |\n| RECOVERY | 80-150K | Revert + re-plan after failure |\n\n**Never mix PLAN and IMPL in same session.**\n\n---\n\n## Layer Manifest\n\n`.claude/manifest.yaml` declares what's harness vs project-biz vs personal. Read at /attach time + drift audit time. Single source of truth for layer membership.\n\n---\n\n## First Interaction\n\nIf user is starting fresh, run `/session-start` to load state and propose next actions.\n```\n\n## agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\n\n```markdown\n# Current Execution — Routing Source of Truth\n\n> THE single source of truth for \"where is work currently happening\".\n> Hardcoded paths in CLAUDE.md or skills are ANTI-PATTERN — they go stale.\n\n## Active Focus Track\n\n**Phase**: 0 — Project Bootstrap (fresh from /attach)\n**Active plan**: (none yet — author after first session)\n**Session N**: S0 (init)\n**Status**: Fresh project; run /session-start to begin\n**autonomous_mode**: false\n\n---\n\n## Routing Table\n\n| Prompt pattern | Resolves to |\n|---|---|\n| (populate as project develops) | (path) |\n\n---\n\n## Current Work Items\n\n(None — populate after first session)\n\n---\n\n## How Agent Uses This File\n\nOn `/session-start`:\n1. Read this file first\n2. Identify active track\n3. Load appropriate plan file\n4. Determine session type\n5. Propose next action\n```\n\n## agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md (harness baseline)\n\n```markdown\n# agent-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> Audience: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\n> Generated from harness baseline by /attach.\n\n## Identity\n\n`agent-workspace/` is the agent-owned execution + memory layer. Agents WRITE here freely\n(within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\n## Subdirectories (skeletal — populate as needed)\n\n- `memory/sessions/` — one file per session\n- `memory/decisions/` — sequential ADRs\n- `memory/checkpoints/` — handoff state for self-reboot\n- `memory/observations/` — subagent return artifacts\n- `memory/agent-notes.md` — learned rules\n- `session-plans/pending/` — plans authored, awaiting execution\n- `session-plans/completed/` — executed plans\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. `current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth — never hardcode paths in skills/CLAUDE.md\n2. `memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status\n3. Provenance is mandatory — every decision references its source\n4. Agents never `git commit` unless user explicitly requests\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\n1. `memory/current-execution.md`\n2. `memory/project.md`\n3. `memory/checkpoints/latest.md` if recent\n4. `memory/agent-notes.md`\n5. Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`\n6. `session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`\n7. Relevant skills + agents + commands as task demands\n```\n\n## human-workspace/CLAUDE.md (harness baseline)\n\n```markdown\n# human-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> Audience: agents reading from + writing into `human-workspace/`.\n> Generated from harness baseline by /attach.\n\n## Identity\n\n`human-workspace/` is the human-owned input + decision layer.\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n- `user_prompt/` — humans drop new prompts here (agent: read-only)\n- `decisions/` — humans author binding decisions (agent: read-only)\n- `q-and-a/pending/` — agent writes Q&A bundles awaiting human reply\n- `q-and-a/answered/` — humans MOVE answered bundles here (agent: read-only)\n- `q-and-a/stale/` — hook auto-moves expired bundles\n- `notifications/` — agent writes URGENT alerts to human\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. Agent NEVER writes to `user_prompt/`, `decisions/`, `q-and-a/answered/`, `q-and-a/stale/`\n2. Agent ONLY writes to `q-and-a/pending/` and `notifications/`\n3. Q&A bundles in `pending/` are pure audit trail; primary input surface is AskUserQuestion tool\n4. File-edit is NOT a reliable input channel for mobile-remote scenarios\n```\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":31,"oldLines":6,"newStart":31,"newLines":14,"lines":[" "," ---"," ","+## Harness Self-Verify Discipline (Charter Principle 11)","+","+**Every hook ships with a companion firing-test in `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/`.** The continuous `harness-health-self-scan` hook verifies signal-set HH-1 through HH-N (codified in `agent-workspace/constitution/harness-health-protocol.md`) on every UserPromptSubmit + SessionStart event. Ritual closure of a track (file existence + smoke-test exit 0) is forbidden until empirical-firing evidence (production log entry / artifact / telemetry row from real session activity) is captured.","+","+This is the inversion of the \"ritual GREEN audit\" anti-pattern: existence + unit-smoke do NOT prove the hook fires in production. Only a real `.session-hooks.log` entry from a real session does.","+","+---","+"," ## Sandwich Pattern (Architect → Dev → Verifier)"," "," Three-persona discipline. Each session adopts ONE persona; never mix."]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cf66b086-f42b-40ea-ac79-455160ede242","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5d445a8b-bf27-47e3-8d5e-cf5f511500fa","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"334fd1c3-0671-4373-9a8b-963dd4b81ba8","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- empirical-probe-first: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability.
